        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_wh_gate, {const: true}]}
          then: f5695.h_23d_capped
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_wh_gate, {const: false}]}
          then: f5695.h_zero
    shown_when: f5695.h_sec_b_form

  - id: f5695.line24c
    printed: "24c"
    label: "Add lines 24a and 24b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f5695.line24a, f5695.line24b]}
    shown_when: f5695.h_sec_b_form

  - id: f5695.h_furnace_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Section B property may be claimed and a valid QMID is provided for each furnace or boiler entered (or none)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_sec_b, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line24c, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.qmid_furnace, {const: true}]}

  - id: f5695.h_24d_product
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 24c by 30% (0.30)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f5695.line24c, rate: {param: f5695.eehi_rate}}

  - id: f5695.h_24d_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do not enter more than $600"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f5695.h_24d_product, f5695.h_item_max]}

  - id: f5695.line24d
    printed: "24d"
    label: "Multiply line 24c by 30% (0.30). Enter the results. Do not enter more than $600"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_furnace_gate, {const: true}]}
          then: f5695.h_24d_capped
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_furnace_gate, {const: false}]}
          then: f5695.h_zero
    shown_when: f5695.h_sec_b_form

  - id: f5695.h_show25
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 21a, 21b and 25a are Yes (lines 25b through 25e are completed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_sec_b_form, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line25a, {const: true}]}

  - id: f5695.h_enabling_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Enabling property may be claimed with valid QMIDs (or no enabling property costs)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_sec_b, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line25a, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line25c, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.qmid_enabling, {const: true}]}

  - id: f5695.h_25e_product
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 25c by 30% (0.30)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f5695.line25c, rate: {param: f5695.eehi_rate}}

  - id: f5695.h_25e_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do not enter more than $600"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f5695.h_25e_product, f5695.h_item_max]}

  - id: f5695.line25e
    printed: "25e"
    label: "Multiply line 25c by 30% (0.30). Enter the results. Do not enter more than $600"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_enabling_gate, {const: true}]}
          then: f5695.h_25e_capped
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_enabling_gate, {const: false}]}
          then: f5695.h_zero
    shown_when: f5695.h_show25

  - id: f5695.h_audit_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$150"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5695.audit_max}

  - id: f5695.h_26c_product
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 26b by 30% (0.30)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f5695.line26b, rate: {param: f5695.eehi_rate}}

  - id: f5695.h_26c_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do not enter more than $150"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f5695.h_26c_product, f5695.h_audit_max]}

  - id: f5695.line26c
    printed: "26c"
    label: "Multiply line 26b by 30% (0.30). Enter the results. Do not enter more than $150"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.line26a, {const: true}]}
          then: f5695.h_26c_capped
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.line26a, {const: false}]}
          then: f5695.h_zero
    shown_when: f5695.line26a

  - id: f5695.line29g
    printed: "29g"
    label: "Add lines 29a through 29f"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f5695.line29a, f5695.line29b, f5695.line29c, f5695.line29d, f5695.line29e, f5695.line29f]}
    shown_when: f5695.h_sec_b_form

  - id: f5695.h_heat_pump_max
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5695.heat_pump_max}

  - id: f5695.h_hp_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Section B property may be claimed and a valid QMID is provided for each heat pump, heat pump water heater and biomass stove or boiler entered (or none)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_sec_b, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line29g, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.qmid_hp, {const: true}]}

  - id: f5695.h_29h_product
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 29g by 30% (0.30)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f5695.line29g, rate: {param: f5695.eehi_rate}}

  - id: f5695.h_29h_capped
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do not enter more than $2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f5695.h_29h_product, f5695.h_heat_pump_max]}

  - id: f5695.line29h
    printed: "29h"
    label: "Multiply line 29g by 30% (0.30). Enter the results. Do not enter more than $2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_hp_gate, {const: true}]}
          then: f5695.h_29h_capped
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f5695.h_hp_gate, {const: false}]}
          then: f5695.h_zero
    shown_when: f5695.h_sec_b_form

  - id: f5695.h_separate_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Joint return of spouses who owned and lived apart in separate main homes (a separate Form 5695 for each main home, combined on line 30)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.separate_homes, {const: true}]}

  - id: f5695.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Add lines 28 and 29h"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f5695.line28, f5695.line29h]}
    refuses: [f5695.joint_occupancy, f5695.business_use, f5695.h_separate_joint]
    note: "The tool does not compute these cases. Joint occupancy needs credit allocation by fraction (lines 18b to 29h and 28). Business use needs nonbusiness allocation. Separate main homes on a joint return need a form per home, with separate limits combined on line 30. Each is refused here."

  - id: f5695.line32
    printed: "32"
    label: "Energy efficient home improvement credit. Enter the smaller of line 30 or line 31. Also include this amount on Schedule 3 (Form 1040), line 5b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f5695.line30, f5695.line31]}

  - id: f5695.line32a
    printed: "32a"
    label: "If the special rule for joint occupants applies, check here and attach a statement"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.part2_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.joint_occupancy, {const: true}]}
    note: "Instructions: joint occupants claiming the credit must check the box. Read as Part II being claimed and the joint-occupancy answer being Yes. Line 30 refuses that case, so a computed return shows the box unchecked."

  - id: f5695.line32b
    printed: "32b"
    label: "If you live in a condominium or cooperative and have a fractional share of the qualified energy efficiency improvements or residential energy property expenditures, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.part2_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.condo, {const: true}]}

  - id: f5695.part1_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part I (residential clean energy credit) applies: Part I property or technology, or a carryforward from 2024"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.any, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.solar, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.solar_water, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.wind, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.geothermal, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.line5a, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.carry, {const: true}]}
    note: "The pages do not define this name. It is read as whether Part I is completed: the property on lines 1 to 5b, or only a 2024 carryforward (the 'Skip lines 1 through 11' note). Fuel cell property (line 7a) is a screening refusal, so it is not included."

  - id: f5695.part2_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Part II (energy efficient home improvement credit) applies: qualified improvements, energy property or a home energy audit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f5695.any, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.insulation, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.doors, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.windows, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.panel, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.heat_pump, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.hp_water_heater, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.central_ac, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.water_heater, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.furnace, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f5695.biomass, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f5695.audit, {const: true}]}
    note: "The pages do not define this name. It is read as whether the filer has any Part II item: Section A improvements, Section B energy property, or a home energy audit."

  - id: f5695.claiming_part1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Claiming the residential clean energy credit, Form 5695, Part I (Schedule 8812 Credit Limit Worksheet A condition 1c)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f5695.line13, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Open point: whether 'claiming' means a credit is available (line 13) or allowed (line 15). Line 15 depends on line 14, which takes Form 1040 line 19 or Credit Limit Worksheet B, which depends on this answer. Reading 'claiming' as line 13 greater than zero (credit available before the tax-liability limit) avoids that cycle."
```
